Products Highlights (for reference only)
公司產品（僅供參考）


ANDROID SMART E-PAYMENT SOLUTIONS (A-SERIES)
安卓智能電子支付解決方案（A系列）

General features:   Powered by Android operating system and PCI PTS certified. Compact, portable, equipped with a large
                    high resolution touch screen and inbuilt dual cameras. Support various kinds of payment options,
                    including bank card payments, Near Field Communication (“NFC”) contactless and QR code payments.
一般特點：               以安卓作業系統操作，並獲得PCI PTS認証。外型小巧，方便手持，具有特大高清電觸摸屏、內置雙攝
                    像頭。支援各種支付方式，包括銀行卡支付、NFC非觸式及二維碼支付。

Products Highlights (for reference only)
公司產品（僅供參考）


ANDROID SMART ELECTRONIC CASH REGISTER SOLUTIONS (E-SERIES)
安卓智能管理及支付解決方案（E系列）

General Features:   Powered by Android operating system, the all-in-one integrated solution delivers cost efficient and space
                    saving features to merchants. Products are PCI PTS certified. Stylish, large color touch screen, customer-
                    facing display and an inbuilt camera. Support various kinds of payment options including bank card
                    payments, NFC contactless and QR code payments.
一般特點：               以安卓作業系統操作的一體化解決方案，使商戶更節省成本和空間。產品擁有PCI PTS認証。外型時
                    尚，擁有特大彩色觸摸屏、面向顧客的顯示屏、內置攝像頭，支援銀行卡支付，NFC非觸式及二維碼
                    等各種支付方式。

UNATTENDED SOLUTIONS (IM-SERIES & SK-SERIES)
自助服務解決方案（IM系列及SK系列）

General features:   Ideal for self-service sales points such as petrol stations, parking and kiosk. Products are certified with
                    PCI PTS and have met required standards for anti-vandalism and waterproofing. Support various kinds of
                    payment options and connectivity options include 3G, 4G, WiFi and Bluetooth.
一般特點：               適用於加油站、停車場、售貨亭等自助銷售場景。產品獲得PCI PTS認證，符合防爆和防水標準。可支
                    援各種支付方式及3G、4G、WiFi和藍牙多個連接選項。

ANDROID SMART PAYPHONE & PAYTABLET SOLUTIONS (M-SERIES)
安卓智能手機及平板支付解決方案 (M系列)

General Features:   Powered by Android operating system and PCI PTS 6.x certified. The M30 and M8 are the first-ever
                    Android smartphone and Android tablet with integrated payment module, which are perfect for retail
                    and hospitality. Support various kinds of payment options and allow merchants to browse the web, read
                    emails, check social media, make phone calls, take product pictures or record product videos, etc.
一般特點：               以安卓作業系統操作，獲得 PCI PTS 6.x 認証。M30 和 M8 是首款搭載集成支付模組的安卓智能手機
                    和安卓平板，適合零售和餐飲業採用。支援各種支付方式，商戶可瀏覽網頁，查閱電郵、查看社交媒
                    體、通話、拍攝產品相片或錄製產品視頻等。

Risk Management and Internal Control Systems                                 風險管理及內部監控系統
The risk management and internal control systems of the Company              本公司的風險管理及內部監控系統旨在管理
are designed to manage rather than eliminate the risk of failure to          而 非 消 除 未 能 達 成 業 務 目 標 的 風 險，而 且 只 能

absolute assurance against all risk issues. We have employed a bottom-       們 已 採 取 由 下 而 上 的 方 法 以 識 別、評 估 及 減 低
up approach for identification, assessment and mitigation of risk at all     在所有業務層面及各功能範疇上的風險。
business unit levels and across all functional areas.

are transferred to government-recognised recycling companies for                環 境 的 影 響。我 們 近 年 亦 加 強 環 保 辦 公 的 理 念

Key scope                                                                       主要範圍
SOGO regulates the sales of goods by the Group in Hong Kong.                    《貨品售賣條例》規管本集團於香港的貨品售賣。
SOGO provides that, in a contract for the sale of goods by description,         《貨 品 售 賣 條 例 》規 定，憑 貨 品 說 明 售 貨 的 合
there is an implied condition that the goods shall correspond with the          約，均有貨品必須與貨品說明相符的隱含條件。
description. It also provides that where a seller sells goods in the course     它 亦 規 定，凡 賣 方 在 業 務 運 作 中 售 貨，有 一 項
of a business, there is an implied condition that the goods supplied            隱含的條件︰根據合約供應的貨品具可商售品
under the contract are of merchantable quality, except where an                 質，除非滿足例外條件。
exception condition is met.

SOSO regulates the supply of services by the Group in Hong Kong.                《服務提供（隱含條款）條例 》規管本集團於香港
SOSO provides that in a contract for the supply of service where the            的服務提供。    《服務提供（隱含條款）條例 》規定，
supplier is acting in the course of a business, there is an implied term        凡 提 供 人 是 在 業 務 過 程 中 行 事，則 在 有 關 的 服
that the supplier will carry out the service with reasonable care and           務提供合約中，即有一隱含條款，規定該人須以
skill. It also provides that under a contract for the supply of a service       合理程度的謹慎及技術作出服務。它亦規定，凡
where the supplier is acting in the course of a business, if the time           服務提供合約就提供人在業務過程中所提供的
for carrying out the service is not fixed by the contract or is not left        服務，沒有訂明作出服務的時間，或非以雙方的
to be determined by the course of dealing between the parties, there            交 易 過 程 來 決 定，則 在 該 合 約 中 即 有 一 隱 含 條
is an implied term that the supplier will carry out the service within a        款，規定提供人須在合理時間內作出該項服務。
reasonable time.

The Company operates a share option scheme adopted on 2 May 2019             本公司運作一項於二零一九年五月二日採納
(“Share Option Scheme”).                                                     的購股權計劃（「購股權計劃」）
                                                                                           。

The purpose of the Share Option Scheme is to reward eligible                 購股權計劃旨在獎勵曾經或將為本集團作出
participants who have contributed or will contribute to the Group and        貢 獻 之 合 資 格 參 與 者，並 鼓 勵 合 資 格 參 與 者 以
to encourage eligible participants to work towards enhancing the value       本 公 司 及 股 東 的 整 體 利 益 為 依 歸，致 力 提 高 本
of the Company and its shares for the benefit of the Company and the         公司及股份的價值。
shareholders as a whole.

Eligible participants of the Share Option Scheme include any Director,       購股權計劃之合資格參與者包括本集團任何
officer, employee or consultant of any member of the Group and any           成 員 公 司 之 任 何 僱 員、董 事、高 級 職 員 或 顧
other groups or classes of participants which the Board, in its absolute     問，及 董 事 會 全 權 酌 情 認 為 已 對 或 將 對 本 集 團
discretion, considers to have contributed or will contribute, whether        之 發 展 及 增 長 作 出 貢 獻（無 論 是 透 過 業 務 聯 盟
by way of business alliance or other business arrangement, to the            或 其 他 業 務 安 排）之 任 何 其 他 團 體 或 參 與 者 類
development and growth of the Group. The Board recognises persons            別。董 事 會 認 可，非 本 集 團 員 工 之 人 士 也 可 以
other than the Group’s personnel may also contribute to the Group’s          本 集 團 分 銷 商、承 包 商、供 應 商、代 理 商、客
development and growth if such persons can contribute in the capacity        戶、業 務 夥 伴 或 其 他 服 務 供 應 商 之 身 份 對 本 集
of distributors, contractors, suppliers, agents, customers, business         團 之 發 展 及 增 長 作 出 貢 獻。董 事 會 將 在 考 慮 並
partners or other service providers of the Group. The Board will apply       非本集團員工之潛在合資格參與者時應用上
the aforesaid in any consideration of potential eligible participants that   文所述。
are not the Group’s personnel.

                                                                                          業 的 認 識 和 外 部 市 場 分 析（如 適 用），佐 證 有 關 存 貨
                                                                                          年期、銷售訂單、過往毛利率及現行市況的解釋。

                                                                                     Based on the procedures described, we found the
                                                                                     estimations of management in relation to the provision
                                                                                     for inventories were supportable by available evidence.
                                                                                     根 據 所 述 程 序，我 們 發 現 管 理 層 有 關 存 貨 撥 備 之 估 計 有
                                                                                     可得的證據支持。

     holding company and together with its subsidiaries (collectively            間 投 資 控 股 公 司，連 同 其 附 屬 公 司（統 稱
     referred to as the “Group”) are principally engaged in the                  「本 集 團」）主 要 從 事 開 發 及 銷 售 電 子 支
     development and sales of electronic funds transfer point-of-sale            付銷售點（「電子支付終端」）產品、提供維
     (“E-payment Terminals”) products, provision of maintenance and              護 及 安 裝 及 支 付 解 決 方 案 服 務（統 稱「電
     installation and payment solution services (collectively, referred to       子支付終端解決方案業務」）          。
     as the “E-payment Terminals solutions business”).

     The Company is a limited liability company incorporated in                  本公司為一間在百慕達註冊成立的有
     Bermuda. The address of its registered office is Clarendon House,           限 責 任 公 司。本 公 司 註 冊 辦 事 處 的 地 址
     2 Church Street, Hamilton HM 11, Bermuda.                                   為Clarendon House, 2 Church Street,

2    SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING                                       2   主要會計政策概要
     POLICIES
     The principal accounting policies applied in the preparation of             編製該等綜合財務報表所採用的主要會
     these consolidated financial statements are set out below. These            計 政 策 載 列 如 下。除 另 有 說 明 外，該 等 政
     policies have been consistently applied to all the years presented,         策於所有所呈列年度一直貫徹應用。
     unless otherwise stated.

     2.1 Basis of preparation                                                    2.1 編製基準
          The consolidated financial statements of the Company have                  本公司的綜合財務報表已根據所
          been prepared in accordance with all applicable Hong Kong                  有 適 用 之 香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則（「 香
          Financial Reporting Standards (“HKFRSs”) and disclosure                    港 財 務 報 告 準 則」）及 香 港《公 司 條
          requirements of the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance                          例》（第622章）的 披 露 規 定 編 製。除
          Cap. 622. The consolidated financial statements have been                  按公平值計入其他全面收益之金融
          prepared under the historical cost convention, except for                  資 產、按 公 平 值 計 入 損 益 之 金 融 資
          financial asset at fair value through other comprehensive                  產及離職後褔利債務以公平值計量
          income (“FVOCI”), financial assets at fair value through                   外，綜 合 財 務 報 表 已 按 歷 史 成 本 法
          profit or loss (“FVPL”) and post-employment benefit                        編製。
          obligation, which are measured at fair values.

          The preparation of the consolidated financial statements in                編製符合香港財務報告準則的綜合
          conformity with HKFRSs requires the use of certain critical                財務報表須使用若干重要會計估
          accounting estimates. It also requires management to                       計，並 要 求 管 理 層 在 應 用 本 集 團 會
          exercise its judgment in the process of applying the Group’s               計 政 策 時 作 出 判 斷。涉 及 更 高 程 度
          accounting policies. The areas involving a higher degree                   判斷或複雜性的領域或假設及估計
          of judgment or complexity, or areas where assumptions                      對綜合財務報表而言屬重要的領域
          and estimates are significant to the consolidated financial                於下文附註4披露。
          statements are disclosed in Note 4.

      2.12 Offsetting financial instruments                                     2.12 金融工具抵銷
           Financial assets and liabilities are offset and the net amount         倘若存在合法可強制執行的權利以
           is reported in the consolidated balance sheet when there is            抵銷所確認金額及擬按淨額基準結
           a legally enforceable right to offset the recognised amounts           算 或 變 現 資 產 及 同 時 結 算 負 債，則
           and there is an intention to settle on a net basis or realise          金 融 資 產 與 負 債 相 互 抵 銷，而 淨 額
           the asset and settle the liability simultaneously. The legally         於 綜 合 資 產 負 債 表 內 呈 報。法 定 可
           enforceable right must not be contingent on future events              執行權利必須不得依賴未來事件而
           and must be enforceable in the normal course of business               定，而 在 一 般 業 務 過 程 中 以 及 倘 本
           and in the event of default, insolvency or bankruptcy of the           公 司 或 對 手 方 一 旦 出 現 違 約、無 償
           Company or the counterparty.                                           債 能 力 或 破 產 時，這 也 必 須 具 有 約
                                                                                  束力。

    2.23 Provisions                                                         2.23 撥備
        Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present                 當本集團因過往事件承擔現有法律
        legal or constructive obligation as a result of past events;           或 推 定 責 任，而 解 除 責 任 很 有 可 能
        it is probable that an outflow of resources will be required           導 致 資 源 流 出，且 金 額 能 夠 可 靠 計
        to settle the obligation; and the amount has been reliably             算的情況下，便會確認撥備。
        estimated.

        Where there are a number of similar obligations, the                   倘 承 擔 若 干 類 似 責 任，於 釐 定 解 除
        likelihood that an outflow will be required in settlement              責 任 是 否 導 致 資 源 流 出 時，將 以 整
        is determined by considering the class of obligations as a             類 責 任 類 別 為 考 慮。即 使 同 類 責 任
        whole. A provision is recognised even if the likelihood of an          當中任何一項導致資源流出可能性
        outflow with respect to any one item included in the same              甚低，亦會確認撥備。
        class of obligations may be small.

        Provisions are measured at the present value of the                    撥備採用稅前利率按照預期需結算
        expenditures expected to be required to settle the obligation          有 關 責 任 的 支 出 現 值 計 量，該 利 率
        using a pre-tax rate that reflects current market assessments          反映當時市場對金錢時間值和有關
        of the time value of money and the risks specific to the               責 任 固 有 風 險 的 評 估。隨 著 時 間 過
        obligation. The increase in the provision due to passage of            去而增加的撥備確認為利息開支。
        time is recognised as interest expense.

                 Revenue from maintenance and installation and                            裝及支付解決方案服務之收益

                 period as a proportion of the total services to be                       份）確 認，因 為 客 戶 會 在 同 時

    2.26 Leases (as lessee)                                                   2.26 租約（承租人）
         Leases are recognised as a right-of-use asset and a                     於租賃資產可供本集團使用的日
         corresponding liability at the date at which the leased asset           期，就 租 賃 確 認 為 使 用 權 資 產 及 相
         is available for use by the Group.                                      應負債。

         Contracts may contain both lease and non-lease                          合同可能同時包含租賃和非租賃組
         components. The Group allocates the consideration in the                成部分。本集團根據相對獨立價格將
         contract to the lease and non-lease components based on                 合同中的對價分配給租賃和非租賃
         their relative stand-alone prices.                                      部分。

         Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are initially               租賃產生的資產及負債初步以現值
         measured on a present value basis. Lease liabilities include            進 行 計 量。租 賃 負 債 包 括 固 定 租 賃
         the net present value of the fixed lease payments. Lease                付 款 的 淨 現 值。根 據 合 理 確 定 延 續
         payments to be made under reasonably certain extension                  選擇權支付的租賃付款亦計入負債
         options are also included in the measurement of the liability.          計量之內。

         The lease payments are discounted using the interest                    租賃付款採用租賃所隱含的利率予
         rate implicit in the lease. If that rate cannot be readily              以 貼 現。倘 無 法 輕 易 確 定 該 利 率（為
         determined, which is generally the case for leases in the               本集團租賃的一般情況），則使用承
         Group, the lessee’s incremental borrowing rate is used,                 租 人 的 增 量 借 款 利 率，即 個 別 承 租
         being the rate that the individual lessee would have to                 人 在 類 似 經 濟 環 境 中 按 類 似 條 款、
         pay to borrow the funds necessary to obtain an asset of                 抵押及條件借入獲得與使用權資產
         similar value to the right-of-use asset in a similar economic           具有類似價值的資產所需資金而必
         environment with similar terms, security and conditions.                須 支 付 的 利 率。為 釐 定 增 量 借 款 利
         To determine the incremental borrowing rate, the Group,                 率，本 集 團 在 可 能 情 況 下，使 用 個
         where possible, uses recent third-party financing received by           別承租人最近獲得的第三方融資
         the individual lessee as a starting point, adjusted to reflect          為 出 發 點 作 出 調 整，以 反 映 自 獲 得
         changes in financing conditions since third party financing             第 三 方 融 資 以 來 融 資 條 件 的 變 動，
         was received and makes any adjustments specific to the                  及 進 行 特 定 於 租 約 的 調 整，例 如 期
         lease, e.g. term, country, currency and security.                       限、國家、貨幣及抵押。

         Lease payments are allocated between principal and finance              租賃付款於本金及財務費用之間作
         cost. The finance cost is charged to profit or loss over the            出 分 配。財 務 費 用 在 租 賃 期 間 於 損
         lease period so as to produce a constant periodic rate of               益 扣 除，藉 以 令 各 期 間 的 負 債 餘 額
         interest on the remaining balance of the liability for each             的期間利率一致。
         period.

      3.2 Capital management                                                     3.2 資本管理
          The Group regards its shareholders’ equity as capital. The               本 集 團 將 其 股 東 權 益 視 為 資 本。本
          Group’s objectives when managing capital are to safeguard                集團管理資本的目的為保障本集團
          the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern in order              繼續以持續經營方式為股東提供回
          to provide returns for shareholders and benefits for other               報以及為其他利益相關者帶來利
          stakeholders and to maintain an optimal capital structure to             益，並 且 維 持 最 佳 資 本 結 構 以 減 少
          reduce the cost of capital.                                              資金成本。

          In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the                為 維 持 或 調 整 資 本 結 構，本 集 團 可
          Group may adjust the amount of dividends paid to                         調 整 向 股 東 支 付 股 息 的 金 額、將 資
          shareholders, return capital to shareholders, issue new                  本 退 還 予 股 東、發 行 新 股 份、回 購
          shares, repurchase the Company’s shares or sell assets to                本公司股份或出售資產以減少債
          reduce debt.                                                             項。

          The Group monitors capital on the basis of gearing ratio.                本集團基於資本負債比率監控資
          This ratio is calculated as total debt divided by total capital.         本。該 比 率 按 債 項 總 額 除 以 資 本 總
          Total debt includes borrowings of the Group. Total capital is            額 計 算。債 項 總 額 包 括 本 集 團 借
          calculated as “equity” as shown in the consolidated balance              貸。資 本 總 額 為 列 於 綜 合 資 產 負 債
          sheet plus total debt.                                                   表的「權益」加上債項總額計算。

          The Group did not have any borrowings as at 31 December                  本集團於二零二一年十二月三十一
          2021 and the gearing ratio is not applicable (2020: same).               日 並 無 借 貸，資 本 負 債 比 率 不 適 用
                                                                                   （二零二零年：同上）     。

4   CRITICAL ACCOUNTING ESTIMATES AND                                       4   重要會計估計及判斷
    JUDGMENTS
    Estimates and judgments used in preparing the consolidated                  用 於 編 製 綜 合 財 務 報 表 的 估 計 及 判 斷，
    financial statements are evaluated based on historical experience           乃 基 於 過 往 經 驗 及 其 他 因 素，包 括 預 期
    and other factors, including expectations of future events that are         日後在有關情況下相信會合理出現的事
    believed to be reasonable under the circumstances.                          件而作出。

    The Group makes estimates and assumptions concerning the                    本 集 團 作 出 有 關 未 來 的 估 計 及 假 設。按
    future. The resulting accounting estimates will, by definition,             此 規 範，所 作 的 會 計 估 計 甚 少 與 有 關 的
    seldom equal to the related actual results. The estimates and               實 際 結 果 相 同。下 文 載 列 有 極 大 風 險 會
    assumptions that have a significant risk of causing a material              導致須對下一個財政年度資產與負債的
    adjustment to the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities within         賬面值作出重大調整的估計及假設。
    the next financial year are addressed below.

    (a)   Impairment of receivables                                             (a)   應收款項減值
          The Group makes provision for impairment of receivables                     本集團基於對違約風險及預期信貸
          based on assumptions about risk of default and expected                     虧損率之假設對應收款項減值計提
          credit loss rates (Note 3.1(b)(ii)). The Group uses judgment                撥備（附註3.1(b)(ii) ）。於結算日，本
          in making these assumptions and selecting the inputs to                     集團根據集團過往記錄及當前市況
          the impairment calculation, based on the Group’s past                       以 及 前 瞻 性 估 計，判 斷 該 等 假 設 及
          history, existing market conditions as well as forward-looking              選擇計算減值之輸入數據。
          estimates at the balance sheet date.

    (b) Provision for inventories                                               (b)   存貨撥備
          Inventories are carried at the lower of cost and net realisable             存貨乃按成本及可變現淨值兩者
          value. The cost of inventories is written down to net                       之 間 較 低 者 入 賬。倘 有 客 觀 證 據 顯
          realisable value when there is an objective evidence that                   示 存 貨 成 本 或 不 能 收 回，則 將 存 貨
          the cost of inventories may not be recoverable. The cost of                 成 本 撇 減 至 可 變 現 淨 值。倘 存 貨 損
          inventories may not be recoverable if those inventories are                 壞、全 部 或 部 份 陳 舊 或 售 價 下 跌，
          aged and damaged, if they have become wholly or partially                   則 可 能 不 能 收 回 存 貨 成 本。倘 進 行
          obsolete, or if their selling prices have declined. The cost                銷 售 將 予 產 生 的 估 計 成 本 增 加，亦
          of inventories may also not be recoverable if the estimated                 可能導致不能收回存貨成本。
          costs to be incurred to make the sales have increased.
          The amount written off in the consolidated income                           於綜合收益表撇銷的金額為存貨的
          statement is the difference between the carrying value                      賬 面 值 與 可 變 現 淨 值 之 間 的 差 額。
          and net realisable value of the inventories. In determining                 於釐定可收回的存貨成本時須作出
          whether the inventories can be recoverable, significant                     重 大 判 斷。於 作 出 有 關 判 斷 時，本
          judgment is required. In making this judgment, the Group                    集 團 會 評 估（其 中 包 括）以 任 何 方 式
          evaluates, among other factors, the duration and extent by                  收回金額的時間及情況。
          all means to which the amount will be recovered.

    (c)   Income taxes and deferred taxation                                    (c)   所得稅及遞延稅款
          Significant judgment is required in determining the provision               對 所 得 稅 釐 定 撥 備 時，需 要 作 出 重
          for income tax. There are many transactions and calculations                要 的 判 斷。許 多 交 易 及 計 算 的 最 終
          for which the ultimate determination is uncertain. Where                    釐 定 無 法 確 定。當 最 終 的 稅 款 結 果
          the final tax outcome of these matters is different from the                與 最 初 記 賬 金 額 不 同 時，有 關 差 額
          amounts that were initially recorded, such difference will                  將影響釐定期間的當期稅項和遞延
          impact the current tax and deferred income tax assets and                   所得稅資產及負債。
          liabilities in the period in which such determination is made.

          Deferred income tax assets relating to certain temporary                    當管理層認為將來很有可能有應課
          differences and tax losses are recognised when management                   稅溢利以扺銷暫時性差額或可使用
          considers it is probable that future taxable profit will be                 稅 務 虧 損 時，有 關 若 干 暫 時 性 差 額
          available against which the temporary differences or tax                    及稅務虧損的遞延所得稅資產予以
          losses can be utilised. The outcome of their actual utilisation             確認。實際使用的結果可能不同。
          may be different.

         negotiated on an individual basis and contain a wide range                款 乃 個 別 磋 商，包 含 各 種 不 同 的 條

         not impose any covenants other than the security interests                的 租 賃 資 產 的 保 證 權 益 外，並 無 任

         may not be used as security for borrowing purposes.                       擔保。

         in the Group’s operations.                                                盡量增加營運彈性。

    CGUs, which represent the lowest level within the Group at which       現金產生單位指就內部管理目的而對相關

    purposes.

    As at 31 December 2021, the Group’s goodwill of HK$14,582,000          於 二 零 二 一 年 十 二 月 三 十 一 日，本 集 團
    (2020: HK$15,743,000) is attributable to the acquisition of the        的 商 譽 為14,582,000港 元（二 零 二 零 年：
    operation in Italy.                                                    15,743,000港元)來自收購意大利的業務。

    The recoverable amounts of CGUs are determined based on value          現金產生單位的可收回款額基於使用價值
    in use calculations. The calculations are performed by an external     計算釐定。該計算根據管理層所批准的五
    valuer by using pre-tax cash flow projection based on financial        年期財政預算由外部估值師採用稅前現金
    budgets approved by management covering a five-year period.            流 量 預 測 進 行 計 算。此 後，推 斷 現 金 流 量
    Thereafter, the cash flows are extrapolated using the terminal         所採用的終端增長率不會超過現金產生單
    growth rates not exceeding the long-term average growth rate of        位經營所在國家的長期平均增長率。
    the country in which the CGU operates.

    Impairment test for goodwill arising from the                          就意大利的業務所產生的商譽進行減
    operation in Italy                                                     值測試
    The key assumptions are as follows:                                    主要假設如下：

                                                                                      2021             2020
                                                                                   二零二一年            二零二零年

    Pre-tax discount rate                    稅前貼現率                                      18.3%            20.6%
    Compound annual growth rate              複合年增長率                                      1.3%             3.1%
    Terminal growth rate                     終端增長率                                       2.0%             2.0%


    The recoverable amount of this CGU is calculated based on the          該現金產生單位的可收回金額根據使用

                                                                                                保持有效及具效力。

                 The purpose of the Share Option Scheme is to reward                            購股權計劃旨在回饋曾或將對
                 eligible participants who have contributed or will                             本 集 團 作 出 貢 獻 的 員 工，並 鼓
                 contribute to the Group and to encourage eligible                              勵參與者為本公司及其股東的
                 participants to work towards enhancing the value of                            整 體 利 益，努 力 提 升 本 公 司 及
                 the Company and its shares for the benefit of the                              其股份的價值。
                 Company and its shareholders as a whole.

33 RELATED PARTY TRANSACTIONS                                              33 關聯方交易
   Parties are considered to be related if one party has, directly           倘一名人士有能力直接或間接控制其他
   or indirectly, the ability to control the other party or exercise         人士或在其他人士作出財務及經營決策
   significant influence over the other party in making financial and        時 對 其 施 加 重 大 影 響，則 該 人 士 被 認 為
   operating decisions.                                                      是該名其他人士的關聯方。
